Dear Mr. (Ms.)______:

As an experienced product design engineer with a successful background supporting and leading product design, engineering, and manufacturing functions, I have dedicated my career to driving superior product development. As your next Senior Mechanical Design Engineer, such is the focus that I will bring to (Company Name).

As the enclosed resume illustrates, my skills and strengths extend to:

** Proven track record of collaboration across global operations and business units to lead the end-to-end product lifecycle spanning design, development, testing, and launch.

** Broad-based knowledge of prevailing government regulatory, safety, and quality, standards, requirements, and guidelines, as well as key industry standards.

** Demonstrated acumen in meeting technical and functional requirements of a project; support the entire design and validation lifecycle and ensure the highest levels of product quality.

** Team building, communication, and leadership acumen, developing training and instructional programs and designing highly effective, safety-focused manufacturing operations.

Throughout my career, I have held responsibility for an array of key functions and teams within environments of continuously shifting business priorities; my experience encompasses a track record of versatility and success managing initiatives from concept to delivery, with demonstrated expertise in developing strategies to propel key performance indicators. As a key leader within (Company Name), I will deliver a comparable track record of achievement, while remaining focused on aligning all functions and activities to short- and long-range strategies.

In closing, I look forward to personally discussing my qualifications and candidacy for the role of (Job Title). Thank you for your consideration.

Sincerely,
Erik Norris

======
Resume
======

Senior Mechanical Design Engineer
=================================
Results-oriented lead mechanical designer with extensive experience in consumer product design, development, and manufacturing. Cross-functional skills include product and project management, and NPI process.

Direct product design, development, and manufacturing in alignment with business objectives, regulatory requirements, quality / safety standards, and manufacturing logistics. Design and engineering expertise with a focus on innovative product design and development of process solutions to increase performance, control costs, and reduce defects. Strong communicator and negotiator; oversee product and project management lifecycles, building and maintaining partner and customer relations. 

Technical expertise includes:
----------------------------
Design: Polymer Parts (Injection Molding, Blow Molding, Vacuum Forming), Sheet Metal Parts (Progressive Die, Four-Slide, CNC Forming / Stamping), Metal Parts (Extrusion, Die Cast, Investment Cast, Powdered Metal, Machined), Design for Testability (DFT), Design for Manufacturability (DFM), Design for Assembly (DFA), Complex / Organic Surfacing, Design Verification Test (DVT), Material Property Analysis / Material Selection, Shock & Vibration Effects, EMI / ESD Design Parameters

Engineering: Prototyping, Design Analysis, Geometric Design & Tolerancing (GD&T), Agency Certification, Cost Reduction / Cost-of-Goods (CoGs) Analysis

Development: Design Optimization, Project Management, Feasibility Studies, Product Specifications, Supply Chain Management, Partner Relations, Supplier Qualification / Negotiation, Bill of Materials (BOM)

Standards: Engineering: ANSI, ISO/IEC, NEMA, IEEE-SA  |  Regulatory: UL, TUV, CSA, CE, FCC, FDA

Manufacturing: Process Design, Process Documentation, Quality Control, Testing (EMC, Radiated Emissions, Conducted Immunity, Thermal, Acoustic, etc.)

Technology: PTC Pro/ENGINEER  Certified Engineering Provider & Professional, MCAD Tools, Flotherm

Professional Experience
=======================
Kaleidescape, Sunnyvale, California, 2008  Present
Senior Mechanical Design Engineer
Serve as primary design engineer for development of high-end entertainment product suites.
Managed all aspects of mechanical design for new products, providing design-related direction across the entire product development and enhancement lifecycle; hold industrial design responsibilities, including concept generation, sketches, renderings, prototyping, and design implementation. Identify, diagnose, and resolve root cause of emerging production issues. Manage manufacturing partner relations to ensure product development in alignment with established standards. 

Selected Contributions:

** Evaluate existing products for cost-reduction or manufacturing process improvement opportunities.

** Product Development: Kaleidescape Mini System, 1080p Mini Player, Blu-Ray Player  and Mini Blu-Ray Player, and 400-disc Blu-Ray changer.

Digidesign, Daly City, California, 2003  2008
Senior Mechanical Designer
Led an array of product design and engineering efforts for leading digital audio product provider.
Directed multiple, concurrent product design efforts, assuming project management-level responsibility and performing ongoing implementation of process improvements. Served as system administrator and subject matter expert for Pro/ENGINEER and Pro/INTRALINK (Windchill). 

Selected Contributions:

** Forged improved documentation and workflow design across all departmental operations.

** Built department Intranet portal facilitating knowledge exchange; enabled design review by non-CAD users.

** Product Development: ICON tactile worksurface and modular furniture system, D-Command tactile worksurface, AVoption|V10 rack-mounted Pro Tools|HD video accessory, and Mbox2 personal studio.

Mechanistic, San Jose, California, 1999  2003
Senior Mechanical Designer
Conceptualized, built, and managed product design consultancy, serving client engagements spanning product concept through manufacturing for global, high-volume production.
Directed design, development, and launch efforts spanning the entire lifecycle of R&D, design, testing, quality assurance, and manufacturing. Held project management responsibilities, integrating into client infrastructure to direct project teams comprised of client professionals and third-party stakeholders. Partnered with industrial design teams to maintain design intent for surface, gap, and material finish requirements; collaborated throughout the organization to plan, coordinate, and execute design / engineering, process development, and product rollout efforts. Ensured consistent adherence to project budget and schedule objectives. 

Selected Contributions:

** Won high-profile clients through consistent demonstration of value proposition and cradle-to-grave product development expertise; clients included Microsoft, Motorola, HP, SGI, and EP Technologies.

** Leveraged documentation standard and product qualification regulation expertise to ensure viable product development; utilized DFM and DFA best practice for high- and low-volume products.

** Liaised with and qualified suppliers and manufacturers to design and implement manufacturing processes, quality control systems, and cost management frameworks.

** Developed high-caliber revision control and change management procedures for CAD deliverables, utilizing Pro/PDM, Pro/INTRALINK (now Windchill) to manage product data.

** Product Development: Microsoft Xbox, Microsoft Ultimate TV Media Center, Motorola MP3 Player, Netscreen 500, HP Jornada 430 PDA, SGI 540 Visual Workstation, DVDO Portable DVD Player, Echelon LonWorks network controllers, WebTV Media Center / Receivers, Sunbeam Toast Logic Window Toaster, Acer Aspire Accessory Telephone, Frantic Frames inline speed skate frames, and SCCA SoloII race car parts.

frogdesign, Sunnyvale, California, 1997  1999
Mechanical Designer
Played key role within design consultancy, serving product development from conceptualization to shipping.
Performed product design, prototyping and feasibility studies, testing, and manufacturing ramp. Provided project coordination and requirement conflict evaluation beyond the design scope, including manufacturing, distribution, and market conflicts. 

Selected Contributions:

** Spearheaded cross-functional collaborations to successfully transfer new designs to high-volume manufacturing facilities; partnered with industrial design to ensure final product alignment with subtle requirements for surface, gap, and material finish. 

** Product Development: KaVo X-Unit / Edeca Dental System, Newscorp Satellite TV Receiver, Vadem Clio.

*** Earlier experience as Mechanical Designer with Silicon Graphics (Mountain View, CA), contributing to Indy, Indigo, and Iris product suite; as Production Supervisor with EP Technologies (Mountain View, CA), in engineering and manufacturing of catheter technologies; and as Tool Technician with De Anza College (Cupertino, CA). ***

De Anza College, Cupertino, California

Senior Mechanical Designer

Directed multiple, concurrent product design efforts, assuming project management-level responsibility and performing ongoing implementation of process improvements. Served as system administrator and subject matter expert for Pro/ENGINEER and Pro/INTRALINK (Windchill). 

Selected Contributions:

** Forged improved documentation and workflow design across all departmental operations.

** Built department Intranet portal facilitating knowledge exchange; enabled design review by non-CAD users.

** Product Development: ICON tactile worksurface and modular furniture system, D-Command tactile worksurface, AVoption|V10 rack-mounted Pro Tools|HD video accessory, and Mbox2 personal studio.

Mechanistic, San Jose, California, 1999  2003
Senior Mechanical Designer
Conceptualized, built, and managed product design consultancy, serving client engagements spanning product concept through manufacturing for global, high-volume production.
Directed design, development, and launch efforts spanning the entire lifecycle of R&D, design, testing, quality assurance, and manufacturing. Held project management responsibilities, integrating into client infrastructure to direct project teams comprised of client professionals and third-party stakeholders. Partnered with industrial design teams to maintain design intent for surface, gap, and material finish requirements; collaborated throughout the organization to plan, coordinate, and execute design / engineering, process development, and product rollout efforts. Ensured consistent adherence to project budget and schedule objectives. 

Selected Contributions:

** Won high-profile clients through consistent demonstration of value proposition and cradle-to-grave product development expertise; clients included Microsoft, Motorola, HP, SGI, and EP Technologies.

** Leveraged documentation standard and product qualification regulation expertise to ensure viable product development; utilized DFM and DFA best practice for high- and low-volume products.

** Liaised with and qualified suppliers and manufacturers to design and implement manufacturing processes, quality control systems, and cost management frameworks.

** Developed high-caliber revision control and change management procedures for CAD deliverables, utilizing Pro/PDM, Pro/INTRALINK (now Windchill) to manage product data.

** Product Development: Microsoft Xbox, Microsoft Ultimate TV Media Center, Motorola MP3 Player, Netscreen 500, HP Jornada 430 PDA, SGI 540 Visual Workstation, DVDO Portable DVD Player, Echelon LonWorks network controllers, WebTV Media Center / Receivers, Sunbeam Toast Logic Window Toaster, Acer Aspire Accessory Telephone, Frantic Frames inline speed skate frames, and SCCA SoloII race car parts.

frogdesign, Sunnyvale, California, 1997  1999
Mechanical Designer
Played key role within design consultancy, serving product development from conceptualization to shipping.
Performed product design, prototyping and feasibility studies, testing, and manufacturing ramp. Provided project coordination and requirement conflict evaluation beyond the design scope, including manufacturing, distribution, and market conflicts. 

Selected Contributions:

** Spearheaded cross-functional collaborations to successfully transfer new designs to high-volume manufacturing facilities; partnered with industrial design to ensure final product alignment with subtle requirements for surface, gap, and material finish. 

** Product Development: KaVo X-Unit / Edeca Dental System, Newscorp Satellite TV Receiver, Vadem Clio.

*** Earlier experience as Mechanical Designer with Silicon Graphics (Mountain View, CA), contributing to Indy, Indigo, and Iris product suite; as Production Supervisor with EP Technologies (Mountain View, CA), in engineering and manufacturing of catheter technologies; and as Tool Technician with De Anza College (Cupertino, CA). ***

=======================
Associates Degree in Automotive Engine Performance
De Anza College, Cupertino, California

--------------------------

Erik Norris
1820 Maryland Street * Redwood City, California 94061
650.224.2010 c * erik@mechanistic.com  *  http://www.mechanistic.com

01-05-10

Cisco Systems, Inc.
170 West Tasman Dr.
San Jose, CA 95134
USA

Dear Sir/Madam:

As an experienced product design engineer with a successful background supporting and leading product design, engineering, and manufacturing functions, I have dedicated my career to driving superior product development. As your next Senior Mechanical Engineer, such is the focus that I will bring to Cisco.

As the enclosed resume illustrates, my skills and strengths extend to:

** Proven track record of collaboration across global operations and business units to lead the end-to-end product lifecycle spanning design, development, testing, and launch.

** Broad-based knowledge of prevailing government regulatory, safety, and quality, standards, requirements, and guidelines, as well as key industry standards.

** Demonstrated acumen in meeting technical and functional requirements of a project; support the entire design and validation lifecycle and ensure the highest levels of product quality.

** Team building, communication, and leadership acumen, developing training and instructional programs and designing highly effective, safety-focused manufacturing operations.

Throughout my career, I have held responsibility for an array of key functions and teams within environments of continuously shifting business priorities; my experience encompasses a track record of versatility and success managing initiatives from concept to delivery, with demonstrated expertise in developing strategies to propel key performance indicators. As a key contributor within Cisco, I will deliver a comparable track record of achievement, while remaining focused on aligning all functions and activities to short- and long-range strategies.

In closing, I look forward to personally discussing my qualifications and candidacy for the role of Senior Mechanical Engineer.

Thank you for your consideration.

Sincerely,

Erik Norris

======
Resume
======

Senior Mechanical Design Engineer
=================================
Results-oriented lead mechanical designer with extensive experience in consumer product design, development, and manufacturing. Cross-functional skills include product and project management, and NPI process.

Direct product design, development, and manufacturing in alignment with business objectives, regulatory requirements, quality / safety standards, and manufacturing logistics. Design and engineering expertise with a focus on innovative product design and development of process solutions to increase performance, control costs, and reduce defects. Strong communicator and negotiator; oversee product and project management lifecycles, building and maintaining partner and customer relations. 

Technical expertise includes:
----------------------------
Design: Polymer Parts (Injection Molding, Blow Molding, Vacuum Forming), Sheet Metal Parts (Progressive Die, Four-Slide, CNC Forming / Stamping), Metal Parts (Extrusion, Die Cast, Investment Cast, Powdered Metal, Machined), Design for Testability (DFT), Design for Manufacturability (DFM), Design for Assembly (DFA), Complex / Organic Surfacing, Design Verification Test (DVT), Material Property Analysis / Material Selection, Shock & Vibration Effects, EMI / ESD Design Parameters

Engineering: Prototyping, Design Analysis, Geometric Design & Tolerancing (GD&T), Agency Certification, Cost Reduction / Cost-of-Goods (CoGs) Analysis

Development: Design Optimization, Project Management, Feasibility Studies, Product Specifications, Supply Chain Management, Partner Relations, Supplier Qualification / Negotiation, Bill of Materials (BOM)

Standards: Engineering: ANSI, ISO/IEC, NEMA, IEEE-SA  |  Regulatory: UL, TUV, CSA, CE, FCC, FDA

Manufacturing: Process Design, Process Documentation, Quality Control, Testing (EMC, Radiated Emissions, Conducted Immunity, Thermal, Acoustic, etc.)

Technology: PTC Pro/ENGINEER  Certified Engineering Provider & Professional, MCAD Tools, Flotherm

Professional Experience
=======================
Kaleidescape, Sunnyvale, California, 2008  Present
Senior Mechanical Design Engineer
Serve as primary design engineer for development of high-end entertainment product suites.
Managed all aspects of mechanical design for new products, providing design-related direction across the entire product development and enhancement lifecycle; hold industrial design responsibilities, including concept generation, sketches, renderings, prototyping, and design implementation. Identify, diagnose, and resolve root cause of emerging production issues. Manage manufacturing partner relations to ensure product development in alignment with established standards. 

Selected Contributions:

** Evaluate existing products for cost-reduction or manufacturing process improvement opportunities.

** Product Development: Kaleidescape Mini System, 1080p Mini Player, Blu-Ray Player  and Mini Blu-Ray Player, and 400-disc Blu-Ray changer.

Digidesign, Daly City, California, 2003  2008
Senior Mechanical Designer
Led an array of product design and engineering efforts for leading digital audio product provider.
Directed multiple, concurrent product design efforts, assuming project management-level responsibility and performing ongoing implementation of process improvements. Served as system administrator and subject matter expert for Pro/ENGINEER and Pro/INTRALINK (Windchill). 

Selected Contributions:

** Forged improved documentation and workflow design across all departmental operations.

** Built department Intranet portal facilitating knowledge exchange; enabled design review by non-CAD users.

** Product Development: ICON tactile worksurface and modular furniture system, D-Command tactile worksurface, AVoption|V10 rack-mounted Pro Tools|HD video accessory, and Mbox2 personal studio.

Palo Alto, CA 94301

Dear Sir/Madam:

As an experienced product design engineer with a successful background supporting and leading product design, engineering, and manufacturing functions, I have dedicated my career to driving superior product development. As your next Senior Mechanical Design Engineer, such is the focus that I will bring to IDEO.

As the enclosed resume illustrates, my skills and strengths extend to:

** Proven track record of collaboration across global operations and business units to lead the end-to-end product lifecycle spanning design, development, testing, and launch.

** Broad-based knowledge of prevailing government regulatory, safety, and quality, standards, requirements, and guidelines, as well as key industry standards.

** Demonstrated acumen in meeting technical and functional requirements of a project; support the entire design and validation lifecycle and ensure the highest levels of product quality.

** Team building, communication, and leadership acumen, developing training and instructional programs and designing highly effective, safety-focused manufacturing operations.

Throughout my career, I have held responsibility for an array of key functions and teams within environments of continuously shifting business priorities; my experience encompasses a track record of versatility and success managing initiatives from concept to delivery, with demonstrated expertise in developing strategies to propel key performance indicators. As a key leader within IDEO, I will deliver a comparable track record of achievement, while remaining focused on aligning all functions and activities to short- and long-range strategies.

In closing, I look forward to personally discussing my qualifications and candidacy for the role of Senior Mechanical Design Engineer. 

Thank you for your consideration.

Sincerely,

Erik Norris

======
Resume
======

Senior Mechanical Design Engineer
=================================
Results-oriented lead mechanical designer with extensive experience in consumer product design, development, and manufacturing. Cross-functional skills include product and project management, and NPI process.

Direct product design, development, and manufacturing in alignment with business objectives, regulatory requirements, quality / safety standards, and manufacturing logistics. Design and engineering expertise with a focus on innovative product design and development of process solutions to increase performance, control costs, and reduce defects. Strong communicator and negotiator; oversee product and project management lifecycles, building and maintaining partner and customer relations. 

Technical expertise includes:
----------------------------
Design: Polymer Parts (Injection Molding, Blow Molding, Vacuum Forming), Sheet Metal Parts (Progressive Die, Four-Slide, CNC Forming / Stamping), Metal Parts (Extrusion, Die Cast, Investment Cast, Powdered Metal, Machined), Design for Testability (DFT), Design for Manufacturability (DFM), Design for Assembly (DFA), Complex / Organic Surfacing, Design Verification Test (DVT), Material Property Analysis / Material Selection, Shock & Vibration Effects, EMI / ESD Design Parameters

Engineering: Prototyping, Design Analysis, Geometric Design & Tolerancing (GD&T), Agency Certification, Cost Reduction / Cost-of-Goods (CoGs) Analysis

Development: Design Optimization, Project Management, Feasibility Studies, Product Specifications, Supply Chain Management, Partner Relations, Supplier Qualification / Negotiation, Bill of Materials (BOM)

Standards: Engineering: ANSI, ISO/IEC, NEMA, IEEE-SA  |  Regulatory: UL, TUV, CSA, CE, FCC, FDA

Manufacturing: Process Design, Process Documentation, Quality Control, Testing (EMC, Radiated Emissions, Conducted Immunity, Thermal, Acoustic, etc.)

Technology: PTC Pro/ENGINEER  Certified Engineering Provider & Professional, MCAD Tools, Flotherm

Professional Experience
=======================
Kaleidescape, Sunnyvale, California, 2008  Present
Senior Mechanical Design Engineer
Serve as primary design engineer for development of high-end entertainment product suites.
Managed all aspects of mechanical design for new products, providing design-related direction across the entire product development and enhancement lifecycle; hold industrial design responsibilities, including concept generation, sketches, renderings, prototyping, and design implementation. Identify, diagnose, and resolve root cause of emerging production issues. Manage manufacturing partner relations to ensure product development in alignment with established standards. 

Selected Contributions:

** Evaluate existing products for cost-reduction or manufacturing process improvement opportunities.

** Product Development: Kaleidescape Mini System, 1080p Mini Player, Blu-Ray Player  and Mini Blu-Ray Player, and 400-disc Blu-Ray changer.

Digidesign, Daly City, California, 2003  2008
Senior Mechanical Designer
Led an array of product design and engineering efforts for leading digital audio product provider.
Directed multiple, concurrent product design efforts, assuming project management-level responsibility and performing ongoing implementation of process improvements. Served as system administrator and subject matter expert for Pro/ENGINEER and Pro/INTRALINK (Windchill). 

Selected Contributions:

** Forged improved documentation and workflow design across all departmental operations.

** Built department Intranet portal facilitating knowledge exchange; enabled design review by non-CAD users.

** Product Development: ICON tactile worksurface and modular furniture system, D-Command tactile worksurface, AVoption|V10 rack-mounted Pro Tools|HD video accessory, and Mbox2 personal studio.

Mechanistic, San Jose, California, 1999  2003
Senior Mechanical Designer
Conceptualized, built, and managed product design consultancy, serving client engagements spanning product concept through manufacturing for global, high-volume production.
Directed design, development, and launch efforts spanning the entire lifecycle of R&D, design, testing, quality assurance, and manufacturing. Held project management responsibilities, integrating into client infrastructure to direct project teams comprised of client professionals and third-party stakeholders. Partnered with industrial design teams to maintain design intent for surface, gap, and material finish requirements; collaborated throughout the organization to plan, coordinate, and execute design / engineering, process development, and product rollout efforts. Ensured consistent adherence to project budget and schedule objectives. 

Selected Contributions:

** Won high-profile clients through consistent demonstration of value proposition and cradle-to-grave product development expertise; clients included Microsoft, Motorola, HP, SGI, and EP Technologies.

** Leveraged documentation standard and product qualification regulation expertise to ensure viable product development; utilized DFM and DFA best practice for high- and low-volume products.

** Liaised with and qualified suppliers and manufacturers to design and implement manufacturing processes, quality control systems, and cost management frameworks.

** Developed high-caliber revision control and change management procedures for CAD deliverables, utilizing Pro/PDM, Pro/INTRALINK (now Windchill) to manage product data.

** Product Development: Microsoft Xbox, Microsoft Ultimate TV Media Center, Motorola MP3 Player, Netscreen 500, HP Jornada 430 PDA, SGI 540 Visual Workstation, DVDO Portable DVD Player, Echelon LonWorks network controllers, WebTV Media Center / Receivers, Sunbeam Toast Logic Window Toaster, Acer Aspire Accessory Telephone, Frantic Frames inline speed skate frames, and SCCA SoloII race car parts.

frogdesign, Sunnyvale, California, 1997  1999
Mechanical Designer
Played key role within design consultancy, serving product development from conceptualization to shipping.
Performed product design, prototyping and feasibility studies, testing, and manufacturing ramp. Provided project coordination and requirement conflict evaluation beyond the design scope, including manufacturing, distribution, and market conflicts. 

Selected Contributions:

** Spearheaded cross-functional collaborations to successfully transfer new designs to high-volume manufacturing facilities; partnered with industrial design to ensure final product alignment with subtle requirements for surface, gap, and material finish. 

** Product Development: KaVo X-Unit / Edeca Dental System, Newscorp Satellite TV Receiver, Vadem Clio.

*** Earlier experience as Mechanical Designer with Silicon Graphics (Mountain View, CA), contributing to Indy, Indigo, and Iris product suite; as Production Supervisor with EP Technologies (Mountain View, CA), in engineering and manufacturing of catheter technologies; and as Tool Technician with De Anza College (Cupertino, CA). ***

Education & Credentials
=======================
Associates Degree in Automotive Engine Performance
De Anza College, Cupertino, California

Professional Certification: 
--------------------------
PTC Certified Engineering Provider (Level 2) ~ PTC Certified Professional (Level 1)

Keyword Summary
===============
Strategic planning, strategic product planning, engineer, engineering, engineered, design engineer, engineering management, engineering manager, engineering supervision, concept, conceptual, conceptualize, prototype, research and development, R&D, engineering lifecycle, engineering project, engineering process, engineering operations, engineering design, engineering specifications, engineering schematics, engineering proof-of-concept, engineering prototype, product engineer, product design, product development, product testing, product launch, product rollout, quality assurance, quality management, quality control, standards, requirements, compliance, project planning, project management, project coordination, project execution, project completion, project launch, project closure, training, teaching, instruction, coaching, mentoring, supervision, management, leadership, team leadership

Directed design, development, and launch efforts spanning the entire lifecycle of R&D, design, testing, quality assurance, and manufacturing. Held project management responsibilities, integrating into client infrastructure to direct project teams comprised of client professionals and third-party stakeholders. Partnered with industrial design teams to maintain design intent for surface, gap, and material finish requirements; collaborated throughout the organization to plan, coordinate, and execute design / engineering, process development, and product rollout efforts. Ensured consistent adherence to project budget and schedule objectives. 

Selected Contributions:

** Won high-profile clients through consistent demonstration of value proposition and cradle-to-grave product development expertise; clients included Microsoft, Motorola, HP, SGI, and EP Technologies.

** Leveraged documentation standard and product qualification regulation expertise to ensure viable product development; utilized DFM and DFA best practice for high- and low-volume products.

** Liaised with and qualified suppliers and manufacturers to design and implement manufacturing processes, quality control systems, and cost management frameworks.

** Developed high-caliber revision control and change management procedures for CAD deliverables, utilizing Pro/PDM, Pro/INTRALINK (now Windchill) to manage product data.

** Product Development: Microsoft Xbox, Microsoft Ultimate TV Media Center, Motorola MP3 Player, Netscreen 500, HP Jornada 430 PDA, SGI 540 Visual Workstation, DVDO Portable DVD Player, Echelon LonWorks network controllers, WebTV Media Center / Receivers, Sunbeam Toast Logic Window Toaster, Acer Aspire Accessory Telephone, Frantic Frames inline speed skate frames, and SCCA SoloII race car parts.

frogdesign, Sunnyvale, California, 1997  1999
Mechanical Designer
Played key role within design consultancy, serving product development from conceptualization to shipping.
Performed product design, prototyping and feasibility studies, testing, and manufacturing ramp. Provided project coordination and requirement conflict evaluation beyond the design scope, including manufacturing, distribution, and market conflicts. 

Selected Contributions:

** Spearheaded cross-functional collaborations to successfully transfer new designs to high-volume manufacturing facilities; partnered with industrial design to ensure final product alignment with subtle requirements for surface, gap, and material finish. 

** Product Development: KaVo X-Unit / Edeca Dental System, Newscorp Satellite TV Receiver, Vadem Clio.

*** Earlier experience as Mechanical Designer with Silicon Graphics (Mountain View, CA), contributing to Indy, Indigo, and Iris product suite; as Production Supervisor with EP Technologies (Mountain View, CA), in engineering and manufacturing of catheter technologies; and as Tool Technician with De Anza College (Cupertino, CA). ***

Education & Credentials
=======================
Associates Degree in Automotive Engine Performance
De Anza College, Cupertino, California

Professional Certification: 
--------------------------
PTC Certified Engineering Provider (Level 2) ~ PTC Certified Professional (Level 1)

===============

01-06-10

OnLive, Inc. 
181 Lytton Ave. 
Palo Alto, CA 94301 

Dear Sir/Madam:

As an experienced product design engineer with a successful background supporting and leading product design, engineering, and manufacturing functions, I have dedicated my career to driving superior product development. As your next Senior Mechanical Design Engineer, such is the focus that I will bring to OnLive.

As the enclosed resume illustrates, my skills and strengths extend to:

** Proven track record of collaboration across global operations and business units to lead the end-to-end product lifecycle spanning design, development, testing, and launch.

** Broad-based knowledge of prevailing government regulatory, safety, and quality, standards, requirements, and guidelines, as well as key industry standards.

** Demonstrated acumen in meeting technical and functional requirements of a project; support the entire design and validation lifecycle and ensure the highest levels of product quality.

** Team building, communication, and leadership acumen, developing training and instructional programs and designing highly effective, safety-focused manufacturing operations.

Throughout my career, I have held responsibility for an array of key functions and teams within environments of continuously shifting business priorities; my experience encompasses a track record of versatility and success managing initiatives from concept to delivery, with demonstrated expertise in developing strategies to propel key performance indicators. As a key leader within OnLive, I will deliver a comparable track record of achievement, while remaining focused on aligning all functions and activities to short- and long-range strategies.

In closing, I look forward to personally discussing my qualifications and candidacy for the role of Senior Mechanical Design Engineer.
Thank you for your consideration.

Sincerely,

Erik Norris

======
Resume
======

Senior Mechanical Design Engineer
=================================
Results-oriented lead mechanical designer with extensive experience in consumer product design, development, and manufacturing. Cross-functional skills include product and project management, and NPI process.

Direct product design, development, and manufacturing in alignment with business objectives, regulatory requirements, quality / safety standards, and manufacturing logistics. Design and engineering expertise with a focus on innovative product design and development of process solutions to increase performance, control costs, and reduce defects. Strong communicator and negotiator; oversee product and project management lifecycles, building and maintaining partner and customer relations. 

Technical expertise includes:
----------------------------
Design: Polymer Parts (Injection Molding, Blow Molding, Vacuum Forming), Sheet Metal Parts (Progressive Die, Four-Slide, CNC Forming / Stamping), Metal Parts (Extrusion, Die Cast, Investment Cast, Powdered Metal, Machined), Design for Testability (DFT), Design for Manufacturability (DFM), Design for Assembly (DFA), Complex / Organic Surfacing, Design Verification Test (DVT), Material Property Analysis / Material Selection, Shock & Vibration Effects, EMI / ESD Design Parameters

Engineering: Prototyping, Design Analysis, Geometric Design & Tolerancing (GD&T), Agency Certification, Cost Reduction / Cost-of-Goods (CoGs) Analysis

Development: Design Optimization, Project Management, Feasibility Studies, Product Specifications, Supply Chain Management, Partner Relations, Supplier Qualification / Negotiation, Bill of Materials (BOM)

Standards: Engineering: ANSI, ISO/IEC, NEMA, IEEE-SA  |  Regulatory: UL, TUV, CSA, CE, FCC, FDA

Manufacturing: Process Design, Process Documentation, Quality Control, Testing (EMC, Radiated Emissions, Conducted Immunity, Thermal, Acoustic, etc.)

Technology: PTC Pro/ENGINEER  Certified Engineering Provider & Professional, MCAD Tools, Flotherm

Professional Experience
=======================
Kaleidescape, Sunnyvale, California, 2008  Present
Senior Mechanical Design Engineer
Serve as primary design engineer for development of high-end entertainment product suites.
Managed all aspects of mechanical design for new products, providing design-related direction across the entire product development and enhancement lifecycle; hold industrial design responsibilities, including concept generation, sketches, renderings, prototyping, and design implementation. Identify, diagnose, and resolve root cause of emerging production issues. Manage manufacturing partner relations to ensure product development in alignment with established standards. 

Selected Contributions:

** Evaluate existing products for cost-reduction or manufacturing process improvement opportunities.

** Product Development: Kaleidescape Mini System, 1080p Mini Player, Blu-Ray Player  and Mini Blu-Ray Player, and 400-disc Blu-Ray changer.

Digidesign, Daly City, California, 2003  2008
Senior Mechanical Designer
Led an array of product design and engineering efforts for leading digital audio product provider.
Directed multiple, concurrent product design efforts, assuming project management-level responsibility and performing ongoing implementation of process improvements. Served as system administrator and subject matter expert for Pro/ENGINEER and Pro/INTRALINK (Windchill). 

Selected Contributions:

** Forged improved documentation and workflow design across all departmental operations.

** Built department Intranet portal facilitating knowledge exchange; enabled design review by non-CAD users.

** Product Development: ICON tactile worksurface and modular furniture system, D-Command tactile worksurface, AVoption|V10 rack-mounted Pro Tools|HD video accessory, and Mbox2 personal studio.

** Broad-based knowledge of prevailing government regulatory, safety, and quality, standards, requirements, and guidelines, as well as key industry standards.

** Demonstrated acumen in meeting technical and functional requirements of a project; support the entire design and validation lifecycle and ensure the highest levels of product quality.

** Team building, communication, and leadership acumen, developing training and instructional programs and designing highly effective, safety-focused manufacturing operations.

Throughout my career, I have held responsibility for an array of key functions and teams within environments of continuously shifting business priorities; my experience encompasses a track record of versatility and success managing initiatives from concept to delivery, with demonstrated expertise in developing strategies to propel key performance indicators. As a key contributor within Flextronics, I will deliver a comparable track record of achievement, while remaining focused on aligning all functions and activities to short- and long-range strategies.

In closing, I look forward to personally discussing my qualifications and candidacy for the role of Senior Mechanical Engineer. Thank you for your consideration.

Sincerely,

Erik Norris

============
ASCII Resume
============

Senior Mechanical Design Engineer
=================================
Results-oriented lead mechanical designer with extensive experience in consumer product design, development, and manufacturing. Cross-functional skills include product and project management, and NPI process.

Direct product design, development, and manufacturing in alignment with business objectives, regulatory requirements, quality / safety standards, and manufacturing logistics. Design and engineering expertise with a focus on innovative product design and development of process solutions to increase performance, control costs, and reduce defects. Strong communicator and negotiator; oversee product and project management lifecycles, building and maintaining partner and customer relations. 

Technical expertise includes:
----------------------------
Design: Polymer Parts (Injection Molding, Blow Molding, Vacuum Forming), Sheet Metal Parts (Progressive Die, Four-Slide, CNC Forming / Stamping), Metal Parts (Extrusion, Die Cast, Investment Cast, Powdered Metal, Machined), Design for Testability (DFT), Design for Manufacturability (DFM), Design for Assembly (DFA), Complex / Organic Surfacing, Design Verification Test (DVT), Material Property Analysis / Material Selection, Shock & Vibration Effects, EMI / ESD Design Parameters

Engineering: Prototyping, Design Analysis, Geometric Design & Tolerancing (GD&T), Agency Certification, Cost Reduction / Cost-of-Goods (CoGs) Analysis

Development: Design Optimization, Project Management, Feasibility Studies, Product Specifications, Supply Chain Management, Partner Relations, Supplier Qualification / Negotiation, Bill of Materials (BOM)

Standards: Engineering: ANSI, ISO/IEC, NEMA, IEEE-SA  |  Regulatory: UL, TUV, CSA, CE, FCC, FDA

Manufacturing: Process Design, Process Documentation, Quality Control, Testing (EMC, Radiated Emissions, Conducted Immunity, Thermal, Acoustic, etc.)

Technology: PTC Pro/ENGINEER  Certified Engineering Provider & Professional, MCAD Tools, Flotherm

Professional Experience
=======================
Kaleidescape, Sunnyvale, California, 2008  Present
Senior Mechanical Design Engineer
Serve as primary design engineer for development of high-end entertainment product suites.
Managed all aspects of mechanical design for new products, providing design-related direction across the entire product development and enhancement lifecycle; hold industrial design responsibilities, including concept generation, sketches, renderings, prototyping, and design implementation. Identify, diagnose, and resolve root cause of emerging production issues. Manage manufacturing partner relations to ensure product development in alignment with established standards. 

Selected Contributions:

** Evaluate existing products for cost-reduction or manufacturing process improvement opportunities.

** Product Development: Kaleidescape Mini System, 1080p Mini Player, Blu-Ray Player  and Mini Blu-Ray Player, and 400-disc Blu-Ray changer.

Digidesign, Daly City, California, 2003  2008
Senior Mechanical Designer
Led an array of product design and engineering efforts for leading digital audio product provider.
Directed multiple, concurrent product design efforts, assuming project management-level responsibility and performing ongoing implementation of process improvements. Served as system administrator and subject matter expert for Pro/ENGINEER and Pro/INTRALINK (Windchill). 

Selected Contributions:

** Forged improved documentation and workflow design across all departmental operations.

** Built department Intranet portal facilitating knowledge exchange; enabled design review by non-CAD users.

** Product Development: ICON tactile worksurface and modular furniture system, D-Command tactile worksurface, AVoption|V10 rack-mounted Pro Tools|HD video accessory, and Mbox2 personal studio.

Mechanistic, San Jose, California, 1999  2003
Senior Mechanical Designer
Conceptualized, built, and managed product design consultancy, serving client engagements spanning product concept through manufacturing for global, high-volume production.
Directed design, development, and launch efforts spanning the entire lifecycle of R&D, design, testing, quality assurance, and manufacturing. Held project management responsibilities, integrating into client infrastructure to direct project teams comprised of client professionals and third-party stakeholders. Partnered with industrial design teams to maintain design intent for surface, gap, and material finish requirements; collaborated throughout the organization to plan, coordinate, and execute design / engineering, process development, and product rollout efforts. Ensured consistent adherence to project budget and schedule objectives. 

Selected Contributions:

** Won high-profile clients through consistent demonstration of value proposition and cradle-to-grave product development expertise; clients included Microsoft, Motorola, HP, SGI, and EP Technologies.

** Leveraged documentation standard and product qualification regulation expertise to ensure viable product development; utilized DFM and DFA best practice for high- and low-volume products.

** Liaised with and qualified suppliers and manufacturers to design and implement manufacturing processes, quality control systems, and cost management frameworks.

** Developed high-caliber revision control and change management procedures for CAD deliverables, utilizing Pro/PDM, Pro/INTRALINK (now Windchill) to manage product data.

** Product Development: Microsoft Xbox, Microsoft Ultimate TV Media Center, Motorola MP3 Player, Netscreen 500, HP Jornada 430 PDA, SGI 540 Visual Workstation, DVDO Portable DVD Player, Echelon LonWorks network controllers, WebTV Media Center / Receivers, Sunbeam Toast Logic Window Toaster, Acer Aspire Accessory Telephone, Frantic Frames inline speed skate frames, and SCCA SoloII race car parts.

frogdesign, Sunnyvale, California, 1997  1999
Mechanical Designer
Played key role within design consultancy, serving product development from conceptualization to shipping.
Performed product design, prototyping and feasibility studies, testing, and manufacturing ramp. Provided project coordination and requirement conflict evaluation beyond the design scope, including manufacturing, distribution, and market conflicts. 

Selected Contributions:

** Spearheaded cross-functional collaborations to successfully transfer new designs to high-volume manufacturing facilities; partnered with industrial design to ensure final product alignment with subtle requirements for surface, gap, and material finish. 

** Product Development: KaVo X-Unit / Edeca Dental System, Newscorp Satellite TV Receiver, Vadem Clio.

*** Earlier experience as Mechanical Designer with Silicon Graphics (Mountain View, CA), contributing to Indy, Indigo, and Iris product suite; as Production Supervisor with EP Technologies (Mountain View, CA), in engineering and manufacturing of catheter technologies; and as Tool Technician with De Anza College (Cupertino, CA). ***

Education & Credentials
=======================
Associates Degree in Automotive Engine Performance
De Anza College, Cupertino, California

Professional Certification: 
--------------------------
PTC Certified Engineering Provider (Level 2) ~ PTC Certified Professional (Level 1)

Keyword Summary
===============
Strategic planning, strategic product planning, engineer, engineering, engineered, design engineer, engineering management, engineering manager, engineering supervision, concept, conceptual, conceptualize, prototype, research and development, R&D, engineering lifecycle, engineering project, engineering process, engineering operations, engineering design, engineering specifications, engineering schematics, engineering proof-of-concept, engineering prototype, product engineer, product design, product development, product testing, product launch, product rollout, quality assurance, quality management, quality control, standards, requirements, compliance, project planning, project management, project coordination, project execution, project completion, project launch, project closure, training, teaching, instruction, coaching, mentoring, supervision, management, leadership, team leadership
